
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- CBR-CDB ---
what is the total number of patiemts who had crnry athrscl natve vssl?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "CRNRY ATHRSCL NATVE VSSL"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Crnry athrscl natve vssl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------

give me the number of patients who have been diagnosed with tobacco use disorder.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Tobacco use disorder"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'tobacco use disorder']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Tobacco use disorder"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'tobacco use disorder']]}

How many single patients had a diagnosis long title protal hypertension?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Protal hypertension" 
AND DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "SINGLE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'single'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'protal hypertension']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "SINGLE" AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Portal hypertension"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'single'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'portal hypertension']]}
----------

Look for the number of elective hospital admission patients who had a unilateral radical neck dissection procedure.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "ELECTIVE" 
AND PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" = "Unilateral radical neck dissection"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'elective'], ['procedures."long_title"', '=', 'unilateral radical neck dissection']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "ELECTIVE" AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Unilat rad neck dissect"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'elective'], ['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'unilat rad neck dissect']]}
----------

Calculate the number of patients admitted to emergency who had an ld body fluid test by lab
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_LOCATION" = "EMERGENCY ROOM ADMIT" 
AND LAB."FLUID" = "LD Body Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_location"', '=', 'emergency room admit'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'ld body fluid']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND LAB."LABEL" = "LD, Body Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'ld, body fluid']]}
----------

AND LAB."LABEL" = "Manual Reticulocyte Count"

----------
TRUTH:

give the number of patients whose death status is 0 and diagnosis short title is ac diastolic heart failure.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" 
AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "ac diastolic heart failure"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'ac diastolic heart failure']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Ac diastolic hrt failure"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'ac diastolic hrt failure']]}

TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Epistaxis"

provide the number of patients with a diagnoses of personal history of other malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction and anus and are still alive.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Personal history of other malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction and anus" 
AND DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = 0
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'personal history of other malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Hx-rectal & anal malign"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'hx-rectal & anal malign']]}
----------

----------
PREDS:
SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE", PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = "62296"

----------

what is the number of emergency room admitted patients who have diagnosis icd9 code e8798?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_LOCATION" = "EMERGENCY ROOM ADMIT" 
AND DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "E8798"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_location"', '=', 'emergency room admit'], ['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', 'e8798']]}

----------
PREDS:

AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_TYPE" = "ADDITIVE"

FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "NEWBORN PRIMARY DISEASE" 
AND LAB."FLUID" = "Pleural"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'newborn primary disease'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'pleural']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "NEWBORN" AND LAB."FLUID" = "Pleural"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'newborn'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'pleural']]}
----------

Count the number of newborn patients whose lab test fluid is blood
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "1" AND LAB."FLUID" = "Blood"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '1'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'blood']]}
----------
TRUTH:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'newborn'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'blood']]}

How many patients with s/p fall as their primary disease had ascitic fluid lab test?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "S/P Fall" 
AND LAB."FLUID" = "Ascitic Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 's/p fall'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'ascitic fluid']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "S/P FALL" AND LAB."FLUID" = "Ascites"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 's/p fall'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'ascites']]}

AND PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" = "Percutaneous liver aspiration"

Give me the number of patients admitted before the year 2129 and whose lab test name is rbc, pleural.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID

FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMITYEAR" < 2179 
AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = 'Pressure ulcer, stage ii'
GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMITYEAR" < "2179" AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Pressure ulcer, stage II"
PRED_OUT: Counter({(0,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(303,): 1})

Out of total number of patients admitted before the year 2150, how many of them had hemodialysis?
PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
JOIN PROCEDURES ON DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = PROCEDURES."SUBJECT_ID"

AND PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" = 'Hemodialysis'

GOLD_OUT: Counter({(1025,): 1})

FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "60" 
AND PRESCRIPTIONS."FORMULARY_DRUG_CD" = "MORP2I"
GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "62" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."FORMULARY_DRUG_CD" = "MORP2I"
PRED_OUT: Counter({(4839,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(5465,): 1})
